259 @dataclass(frozen=True)
260 class Circle :
261 center: Point
262 radius: float
263
264
265 def circle_from_two(a: Point, b: Point) ‑> Circle:
266 center = (
267 (a[0] + b[0]) / 2.0,
268 (a[1] + b[1]) / 2.0,
269 )
270 return Circle(center, distance(center, a))
271
272
273 def circle_from_three(
274 a: Point,
275 b: Point,
276 c: Point,
277 ) ‑> Optional[Circle]:
278 ax, ay = a
279 bx, by = b
280 cx, cy = c
281
282 denom = 2.0 * (
283 ax * (by ‑ cy)
284 + bx * (cy ‑ ay)
285 + cx * (ay ‑ by)
286 )
287
288 if abs(denom) <= 1e‑12:
289 return None
290
291 a2 = ax * ax + ay * ay
292 b2 = bx * bx + by * by
293 c2 = cx * cx + cy * cy
294
295 ux = (
39
===== P72 =====
B 题程序附录 问题一至问题四源代码
296 a2 * (by ‑ cy)
297 + b2 * (cy ‑ ay)
298 + c2 * (ay ‑ by)
299 ) / denom
300
301 uy = (
302 a2 * (cx ‑ bx)
303 + b2 * (ax ‑ cx)
304 + c2 * (bx ‑ ax)
305 ) / denom
306
307 center = (ux, uy)
308 return Circle(center, distance(center, a))
309
310
311 def circle_contains(circle: Circle, point: Point) ‑> bool:
312 return distance(circle.center, point) <= circle.radius + 1e‑7
313
314
315 def minimum_enclosing_circle(points: list[Point]) ‑> Circle:
316 if not points:
317 raise ValueError (" 空点集没有最小包围圆")
318
319 pts = list(dict.fromkeys(points))
320 random.Random(20260912).shuffle(pts)
321
322 mec: Optional[Circle] = None
323
324 for i, p in enumerate(pts):
325 if mec is not None and circle_contains(mec, p):
326 continue
327
328 mec = Circle(p, 0.0)
329
330 for j in range(i):
331 q = pts[j]
332
333 if circle_contains(mec, q):
334 continue
335
336 mec = circle_from_two(p, q)
337
338 for k in range(j):
339 r = pts[k]
340
341 if circle_contains(mec, r):
342 continue
343
344 circle3 = circle_from_three(p, q, r)
345
346 if circle3 is not None :
347 mec = circle3
348 else:
349 pairs = [
350 circle_from_two(p, q),
351 circle_from_two(p, r),
352 circle_from_two(q, r),
353 ]
354
355 valid_pairs = [
356 item
357 for item in pairs
358 if circle_contains(item, p)
359 and circle_contains(item, q)
360 and circle_contains(item, r)
361 ]
362
363 mec = min(
364 valid_pairs,
365 key=lambda item: item.radius,
366 )
367
368 assert mec is not None
369
370 return Circle(
371 mec.center,
372 max(distance(mec.center, p) for p in pts),
373 )

404 @dataclass(frozen=True)
405 class GeometricProbe :
406 point: Point
407 sin_phi: float
408 error_proxy_m: float
409
410
411 def point_in_convex_polygon(
412 point: Point,
413 polygon: list[Point],
414 eps: float = 1e‑7,
415 ) ‑> bool:
416 if not polygon:
417 return False
418
419 orientation = 0
420
421 for i in range(len(polygon)):
422 a = polygon[i]
423 b = polygon[(i + 1) % len(polygon)]
424
425 value = cross(
426 (b[0] ‑ a[0], b[1] ‑ a[1]),
427 (point[0] ‑ a[0], point[1] ‑ a[1]),
428 )
429
430 if abs(value) <= eps:
431 continue
432
433 edge_sign = 1 if value > 0 else ‑1
434
435 if orientation == 0:
436 orientation = edge_sign
437 elif edge_sign != orientation:
438 return False
439
440 return True

442
443 def bearing_pair_probe(
444 first: "DirectionObservation",
445 second: "DirectionObservation",
446 ) ‑> Optional[GeometricProbe]:
447 angle1 = math.radians(first.bearing_deg)
448 angle2 = math.radians(second.bearing_deg)
449
450 ray1 = (math.cos(angle1), math.sin(angle1))
451 ray2 = (math.cos(angle2), math.sin(angle2))
452
453 denom = cross(ray1, ray2)
454 sin_phi = abs(denom)
455
456 if sin_phi < GEOM_PROBE_MIN_SIN:
457 return None
458
459 offset = (
460 second.station[0] ‑ first.station[0],
461 second.station[1] ‑ first.station[1],
462 )
463
464 dist1 = cross(offset, ray2) / denom
465 dist2 = cross(offset, ray1) / denom
466
467 if dist1 <= 0.0 or dist2 <= 0.0:
468 return None
469
470 point = (
471 first.station[0] + dist1 * ray1[0],
472 first.station[1] + dist1 * ray1[1],
473 )
474
475 err_est = (
476 math.tan(math.radians(BEARING_ERROR_DEG))
477 * (abs(dist1) + abs(dist2))
478 / sin_phi
479 )
480
481 return GeometricProbe(
482 point=point,
483 sin_phi=sin_phi,
484 error_proxy_m=err_est,
485 )

488 def build_backbone() ‑> list[tuple[int, Point]]:
489 sites = [(0.0, 0.0)]
490
491 for i in range(EW_OUTER_COUNT):
492 theta = 2.0 * math.pi * i / EW_OUTER_COUNT
493
41
===== P74 =====
B 题程序附录 问题一至问题四源代码
494 sites.append(
495 (
496 EW_RING_RADIUS * math.cos(theta),
497 EW_RING_RADIUS * math.sin(theta),
498 )
499 )
500
501 return list(enumerate(sites))
502
503
504 def analytic_cover_radius() ‑> float:
505 half_gap = math.pi / EW_OUTER_COUNT
506
507 center_gap = EW_RING_RADIUS / (2.0 * math.cos(half_gap))
508
509 boundary_gap = math.sqrt(
510 TARGET_RADIUS**2
511 + EW_RING_RADIUS**2
512 ‑ 2.0
513 * TARGET_RADIUS
514 * EW_RING_RADIUS
515 * math.cos(half_gap)
516 )
517
518 return max(center_gap, boundary_gap)

545 ) ‑> dict:
546 payload = self.base(request_id)
547
548 payload["position"] = {
549 "x": float(position[0]),
550 "y": float(position[1]),
551 }
552
553 payload["channel"] = int(channel)
554
555 return payload
556
557 def post(self, path: str, payload: dict) ‑> dict:
558 body = json.dumps(
559 payload,
560 ensure_ascii=False,
561 ).encode("utf‑8")
562
563 last_exc: Optional[ Exception] = None
564
565 for retry_no in range(1, HTTP_RETRIES + 1):
566 req = Request(
567 BASE_URL + path,
568 data=body,
569 headers={"Content‑Type": "application/json"},
570 method="POST",
571 )
572
573 try:
574 with urlopen(
575 req,
576 timeout=HTTP_TIMEOUT_S,
577 ) as resp:
578 return json.loads(
579 resp.read().decode("utf‑8")
580 )
581
582 except HTTPError as exc:
583 message = ""
584
585 try:
586 message = exc.read().decode(
587 "utf‑8",
588 errors="replace",
589 )
590 except Exception :
591 pass
592

===== P75 =====
B 题程序附录 问题一至问题四源代码
593 raise RuntimeError (
594 f"HTTP {exc.code}: {message}"
595 ) from exc
596
597 except (
598 URLError,
599 TimeoutError,
600 socket.timeout,
601 ConnectionError,
602 ) as exc:
603 last_exc = exc
604 print(
605 f"[网络异常] attempt= {retry_no}, "
606 f" 复用同一 request_id: {exc}"
607 )
608
609 assert last_exc is not None
610 raise last_exc

1446 @staticmethod
1447 def mandatory_action_id(action):

1449
1450 if kind == "EW":
1451 return ("EW", int(action[1]))

1465 def mandatory_action_point(action) ‑> Point:
1466 kind = action[0]
1467
1468 if kind == "EW":
1469 return action[2]
1470
1471 if kind == "CLEAR":

1476 def _mandatory_open_tsp_first(
1477 self,
1478 nodes: list[tuple[tuple[str, int], Point]],
1479 ) ‑> tuple[
1480 Optional[tuple[str, int]],
1481 float,
1482 dict[tuple[str, int], float],
1483 ]:

===== P84 =====
B 题程序附录 问题一至问题四源代码
1484 n = len(nodes)
1485
1486 if n == 0:
1487 return None , 0.0, {}
1488
1489 points = [point for _, point in nodes]
1490
1491 pair_dist = [
1492 [
1493 distance(points[i], points[j])
1494 for j in range(n)
1495 ]
1496 for i in range(n)
1497 ]
1498
1499 start_dist = [
1500 distance(self.position, point)
1501 for point in points
1502 ]
1503
1504 memo: dict[tuple[int, int], float] = {}
1505
1506 def tail_cost(
1507 last: int,
1508 remaining_mask: int,
1509 ) ‑> float:
1510 if remaining_mask == 0:
1511 return 0.0
1512
1513 key = (last, remaining_mask)
1514
1515 cached = memo.get(key)
1516
1517 if cached is not None :
1518 return cached
1519
1520 best = float("inf")
1521
1522 mask = remaining_mask
1523
1524 while mask:
1525 bit = mask & ‑mask
1526 nxt = bit.bit_length() ‑ 1
1527
1528 candidate = (
1529 pair_dist[last][nxt]
1530 + tail_cost(
1531 nxt,
1532 remaining_mask ^ bit,
1533 )
1534 )
1535
1536 if candidate < best:
1537 best = candidate
1538
1539 mask ^= bit
1540
1541 memo[key] = best
1542 return best
1543
1544 # 状态压缩 DP
1545 full_mask = (1 << n) ‑ 1
1546
1547 forced_first_cost: dict[
1548 tuple[str, int],
1549 float,
1550 ] = {}
1551
1552 best_id: Optional[tuple[str, int]] = None
1553 best_cost = float("inf")
1554
1555 for first in range(n):
1556 remaining = full_mask ^ (1 << first)
1557
1558 total = (
1559 start_dist[first]
1560 + tail_cost(
1561 first,
1562 remaining,
1563 )
1564 )
1565
1566 node_id = nodes[first][0]
1567 forced_first_cost[node_id] = total
1568
1569 if total < best_cost:
1570 best_cost = total
1571 best_id = node_id
1572
1573 return (
1574 best_id,
1575 best_cost,
1576 forced_first_cost,
1577 )

235 def clip_left(
236 polygon: list[Point],
237 line_point: Point,
238 line_direction: Point,
239 eps: float = 1e‑9,
240 ) ‑> list[Point]:
241 if not polygon:
242 return []
243
244 def value(p: Point) ‑> float:
245 return cross(line_direction, sub(p, line_point))
246
247 result: list[Point] = []
248 prev = polygon[‑1]
249 prev_value = value(prev)
250 prev_in = prev_value >= ‑eps
251
252 for cur in polygon:
253 cur_value = value(cur)
254 cur_in = cur_value >= ‑eps
255
256 if prev_in != cur_in:
257 denom = prev_value ‑ cur_value
258 if abs(denom) > 1e‑15:
259 t = prev_value / denom
260 t = min(1.0, max(0.0, t))
261 result.append(
262 (
263 prev[0] + t * (cur[0] ‑ prev[0]),
264 prev[1] + t * (cur[1] ‑ prev[1]),
265 )
266 )
267
268 if cur_in:
269 result.append(cur)
270
271 prev = cur
272 prev_value = cur_value
273 prev_in = cur_in
274
275 clean: list[Point] = []
276 for p in result:
277 if not clean or distance(clean[‑1], p) > 1e‑8:
278 clean.append(p)
279
280 if len(clean) > 1 and distance(clean[0], clean[‑1]) <= 1e‑8:
281 clean.pop()
282
283 return clean

370 def polygon_diameter(
371 polygon: list[Point],
372 ) ‑> tuple[float, tuple[Point, Point]]:
373 if not polygon:
374 raise ValueError (" 空多边形没有直径")
375
376 if len(polygon) == 1:
377 return 0.0, (polygon[0], polygon[0])
378
379 best_sq = ‑1.0
380 best_pair = (polygon[0], polygon[1])
381
382 for i, p in enumerate(polygon):
383 for q in polygon[i + 1:]:
384 sq = (
385 (p[0] ‑ q[0]) ** 2
386 + (p[1] ‑ q[1]) ** 2
387 )
388
389 if sq > best_sq:
390 best_sq = sq
391 best_pair = (p, q)
392
393 return math.sqrt(best_sq), best_pair

396 @dataclass(frozen=True)
397 class Circle :
398 center: Point
399 radius: float
400
401
402 def circle_from_two(a: Point, b: Point) ‑> Circle:
403 center = (
404 (a[0] + b[0]) / 2.0,
405 (a[1] + b[1]) / 2.0,
406 )
407 return Circle(center, distance(center, a))
408
409
410 def circle_from_three(
411 a: Point,
412 b: Point,
413 c: Point,
414 ) ‑> Optional[Circle]:
415 ax, ay = a
416 bx, by = b
417 cx, cy = c
418
419 det = 2.0 * (
420 ax * (by ‑ cy)
421 + bx * (cy ‑ ay)
422 + cx * (ay ‑ by)
423 )
424
425 if abs(det) <= 1e‑12:
426 return None
427
428 a2 = ax * ax + ay * ay
429 b2 = bx * bx + by * by
430 c2 = cx * cx + cy * cy
431
432 ux = (
433 a2 * (by ‑ cy)
434 + b2 * (cy ‑ ay)
435 + c2 * (ay ‑ by)
436 ) / det
437
438 uy = (
439 a2 * (cx ‑ bx)
440 + b2 * (ax ‑ cx)
441 + c2 * (bx ‑ ax)
442 ) / det
443
444 center = (ux, uy)
445 return Circle(center, distance(center, a))
446
447
448 def circle_contains(circle: Circle, point: Point) ‑> bool:
449 return distance(circle.center, point) <= circle.radius + 1e‑7
450
451
452 def minimum_enclosing_circle(points: list[Point]) ‑> Circle:
453 if not points:
454 raise ValueError (" 空点集没有最小包围圆")
455
456 unique = list(dict.fromkeys(points))
457 random.Random(20260912).shuffle(unique)
458
459 circle: Optional[Circle] = None
460
461 for i, p in enumerate(unique):
462 if circle is not None and circle_contains(circle, p):
463 continue
464
465 circle = Circle(p, 0.0)
466
467 for j in range(i):
468 q = unique[j]
469
470 if circle_contains(circle, q):
471 continue
472
473 circle = circle_from_two(p, q)
474
475 for k in range(j):
476 r = unique[k]
477
478 if circle_contains(circle, r):
479 continue
480
481 candidate = circle_from_three(p, q, r)
482
483 if candidate is not None :
484 circle = candidate
485 else:
486 options = [
487 circle_from_two(p, q),
488 circle_from_two(p, r),
489 circle_from_two(q, r),
490 ]
491
492 valid = [
493 item
64
===== P97 =====
B 题程序附录 问题一至问题四源代码
494 for item in options
495 if circle_contains(item, p)
496 and circle_contains(item, q)
497 and circle_contains(item, r)
498 ]
499
500 circle = min(
501 valid,
502 key=lambda item: item.radius,
503 )
504
505 assert circle is not None
506
507 return Circle(
508 circle.center,
509 max(distance(circle.center, p) for p in unique),
510 )

550 def point_in_convex_polygon(
551 point: Point,
552 polygon: list[Point],
553 eps: float = 1e‑7,
554 ) ‑> bool:
555 """ 判断点是否在凸多边形内。"""
556 if not polygon:
557 return False
558
559 sign = 0
560
561 for i in range(len(polygon)):
562 a = polygon[i]
563 b = polygon[(i + 1) % len(polygon)]
564
565 value = cross(
566 (b[0] ‑ a[0], b[1] ‑ a[1]),
567 (point[0] ‑ a[0], point[1] ‑ a[1]),
568 )
569
570 if abs(value) <= eps:
571 continue
572
573 current = 1 if value > 0 else ‑1
574
575 if sign == 0:
576 sign = current
577 elif current != sign:
578 return False
579
580 return True

583 def point_segment_distance(
584 point: Point,
585 a: Point,
586 b: Point,
587 ) ‑> float:
588 """ 点到闭线段的欧氏距离。"""
589 vx = b[0] ‑ a[0]
590 vy = b[1] ‑ a[1]
591 vv = vx * vx + vy * vy
592
65
===== P98 =====
B 题程序附录 问题一至问题四源代码
593 if vv <= 1e‑18:
594 return distance(point, a)
595
596 t = (
597 (point[0] ‑ a[0]) * vx
598 + (point[1] ‑ a[1]) * vy
599 ) / vv
600 t = min(1.0, max(0.0, t))
601
602 projection = (
603 a[0] + t * vx,
604 a[1] + t * vy,
605 )
606 return distance(point, projection)

609 def point_to_convex_polygon_distance(
610 point: Point,

612 ) ‑> float:
613 """ 计算点到凸多边形的最短距离。"""
614 if not polygon:

616

618 return 0.0
619

621 point_segment_distance(

623 polygon[i],

630 def segment_disk_parameter_interval(
631 start: Point,
632 end: Point,
633 center: Point,
634 radius: float,
635 ) ‑> Optional[tuple[float, float]]:
636 """ 返回线段落在给定圆盘内的参数区间。"""
637 dx = end[0] ‑ start[0]
638 dy = end[1] ‑ start[1]
639 fx = start[0] ‑ center[0]
640 fy = start[1] ‑ center[1]
641
642 aa = dx * dx + dy * dy
643 cc = fx * fx + fy * fy ‑ radius * radius
644
645 if aa <= 1e‑18:
646 if cc <= 1e‑12:
647 return 0.0, 1.0
648 return None
649
650 bb = 2.0 * (fx * dx + fy * dy)
651 discriminant = bb * bb ‑ 4.0 * aa * cc
652
653 if discriminant < ‑1e‑10:
654 return None
655
656 discriminant = max(0.0, discriminant)
657 root = math.sqrt(discriminant)
658
659 t1 = (‑bb ‑ root) / (2.0 * aa)
660 t2 = (‑bb + root) / (2.0 * aa)
661
662 lo = max(0.0, min(t1, t2))
663 hi = min(1.0, max(t1, t2))
664
665 if lo > hi + 1e‑12:
666 return None
667
668 return lo, hi

671 def bearing_pair_probe(
672 first: "DirectionObservation",
673 second: "DirectionObservation",
674 ) ‑> Optional[GeometricProbe]:
675 """ 由两条中心示向线生成几何试探点和误差代理。"""
676 a = math.radians(first.bearing_deg)
677 b = math.radians(second.bearing_deg)
678
679 d1 = (math.cos(a), math.sin(a))
680 d2 = (math.cos(b), math.sin(b))
681
682 denominator = cross(d1, d2)
683 sin_phi = abs(denominator)
684
685 if sin_phi < GEOM_PROBE_MIN_SIN:
686 return None
687
688 delta = (
689 second.station[0] ‑ first.station[0],
690 second.station[1] ‑ first.station[1],
691 )
66
===== P99 =====
B 题程序附录 问题一至问题四源代码
692
693 t1 = cross(delta, d2) / denominator
694 t2 = cross(delta, d1) / denominator
695
696 if t1 <= 0.0 or t2 <= 0.0:
697 return None
698
699 point = (
700 first.station[0] + t1 * d1[0],
701 first.station[1] + t1 * d1[1],
702 )
703
704 error_proxy = (
705 math.tan(math.radians(BEARING_ERROR_DEG))
706 * (abs(t1) + abs(t2))
707 / sin_phi
708 )
709
710 return GeometricProbe(
711 point=point,
712 sin_phi=sin_phi,
713 error_proxy_m=error_proxy,
714 )

754 alpha = math.pi / INNER_SEARCH_COUNT

822 def run_geometry_self_test(samples: int = 20000) ‑> None:

857 worst_radius = max(MIN_RECEIVE_RADIUS, float(r_i))

859 for beta_i in range(0, 360, 3):
860 beta = math.radians(beta_i)
861 emission = (math.cos(beta), math.sin(beta))
862 station_visible = (
863 ‑source[0] * emission[0]
864 ‑ source[1] * emission[1]
865 ) >= ‑1e‑10
866 if not station_visible:
867 continue
868
869 recovered = any(
870 distance(point, source) <= worst_radius + 1e‑7
871 and (
872 (point[0] ‑ source[0]) * emission[0]
873 + (point[1] ‑ source[1]) * emission[1]
874 ) >= ‑1e‑7
875 for point in recovery
876 )
877 if not recovered:
878 raise AssertionError ((source, beta_i))

881 (10.0, 0.0),

976 class OfficialClient :
977 def __init__(self) ‑> None:
978 self.request_counter = 0
979
980 def next_request_id(self, prefix: str) ‑> str:
981 self.request_counter += 1
982 return (
983 f"{prefix}‑"
984 f"{int(time.time() * 1000) }‑"
985 f"{self.request_counter}"
986 )

988 def base(self, request_id: str) ‑> dict:
69
===== P102 =====
B 题程序附录 问题一至问题四源代码
989 return {

995 def action_payload(
996 self,
997 request_id: str,
998 position: Point,
999 channel: int,
1000 ) ‑> dict:
1001 payload = self.base(request_id)
1002
1003 payload["position"] = {
1004 "x": float(position[0]),
1005 "y": float(position[1]),
1006 }
1007
1008 payload["channel"] = int(channel)
1009

1011
1012 def post(self, path: str, payload: dict) ‑> dict:
1013 """ 发送请求；重试时保持原 request_id 和请求体。"""
1014 raw_body = json.dumps(
1015 payload,
1016 ensure_ascii=False,
1017 ).encode("utf‑8")
1018
1019 last_error: Optional[ Exception] = None
1020
1021 for attempt in range(1, HTTP_RETRIES + 1):
1022 request = Request(
1023 BASE_URL + path,
1024 data=raw_body,
1025 headers={"Content‑Type": "application/json"},
1026 method="POST",
1027 )
1028
1029 try:
1030 with urlopen(
1031 request,
1032 timeout=HTTP_TIMEOUT_S,
1033 ) as response:
1034 return json.loads(
1035 response.read().decode("utf‑8")
1036 )
1037
1038 except HTTPError as exc:
1039 text = ""
1040
1041 try:
1042 text = exc.read().decode(
1043 "utf‑8",
1044 errors="replace",
1045 )
1046 except Exception :
1047 pass
1048
1049 raise RuntimeError (
1050 f"HTTP {exc.code}: {text}"
1051 ) from exc
1052
1053 except (
1054 URLError,
1055 TimeoutError,
1056 socket.timeout,
1057 ConnectionError,
1058 ) as exc:
1059 last_error = exc
1060 print(
1061 f"[网络异常] attempt= {attempt}, "
1062 f" 复用同一 request_id: {exc}"
1063 )
1064
1065 assert last_error is not None
1066 raise last_error
1067
1068 def enter(self) ‑> dict:
1069 rid = self.next_request_id("enter")
1070 return self.post("/enter", self.base(rid))

1609 def q2_fast_candidates(

1642 def q2_fast_information(
1643 self,
1644 channel: int,
1645 point: Point,
1646 ) ‑> float:
1647 info = self.target_info(channel)
1648 last = self.channels[channel].directions[‑1]
1649
1650 a = (
1651 info.center[0] ‑ last.station[0],
1652 info.center[1] ‑ last.station[1],
1653 )
1654
1655 b = (
1656 info.center[0] ‑ point[0],
1657 info.center[1] ‑ point[1],
1658 )

1661 nb = math.hypot(b[0], b[1])
1662
1663 if na <= 1e‑9 or nb <= 1e‑9:
1664 return 1.0
1665
1666 return min(
1667 1.0,
1668 abs(cross(a, b)) / (na * nb),
1669 )

2025 def build_posterior_sweep(
2026 self,
2027 channel: int,
2028 ) ‑> list[Point]:
2029 """ 后验足够小时生成固定步长的清除网格。"""
2030 polygon = self.channels[channel].polygon
2031 if not polygon:
2032 return []
2033
2034 xmin = min(point[0] for point in polygon)
2035 xmax = max(point[0] for point in polygon)
2036 ymin = min(point[1] for point in polygon)
2037 ymax = max(point[1] for point in polygon)
2038
2039 nx = max(1, math.ceil((xmax ‑ xmin) / SWEEP_GRID_STEP_M))
2040 ny = max(1, math.ceil((ymax ‑ ymin) / SWEEP_GRID_STEP_M))
2041 xs = [xmin + i * SWEEP_GRID_STEP_M for i in range(nx + 1)]
2042 ys = [ymin + i * SWEEP_GRID_STEP_M for i in range(ny + 1)]
2043
2044 points: list[Point] = []
2045 for row, y in enumerate(ys):
2046 row_points = [(x, y) for x in xs]
2047 if row % 2:
2048 row_points.reverse()
2049 points.extend(row_points)
2050
2051 # Sweep 保持原 V5 顺序。
2052 return points

2477 def plan_current_search_phase(self) ‑> None:

2642 def best_future_search_insertion_detour(
2643 self,
2644 target: Point,
2645 route: Optional[list[tuple[int, Point]]] = None,
2646 ) ‑> float:
2647 """ 估计目标点插入后续搜索路线的最小绕路。"""

2651 return 0.0

2653 previous = route[0][1]
2654 best = math.inf
2655
2656 for _, next_point in route[1:]:
2657 detour = (
2658 distance(previous, target)
2659 + distance(target, next_point)
2660 ‑ distance(previous, next_point)
2661 )
2662 best = min(best, max(0.0, detour))
2663 previous = next_point
2664
2665 # 最后一个 EW 后直接接目标点。
2666 best = min(best, distance(previous, target))
